# Live Misebox Kitchen and Item data audit

- Firebase project `misebox-78f9c`
- Firestore database `misebox` (Standard, Firestore Native, `eur3`)
- Environment live production, read-only
- Snapshot read time `2026-11-23T11:45:06Z` (2026-11-23 12:45:06 CET)

All document and collection listings use the same Firestore read time. Each record includes its
path and Firestore document timestamps alongside its stored fields. JSON numbers preserve
Firestore integers as whole numbers and doubles with a decimal point or exponent. Timestamp
fields use `{"$timestampValue":"…"}` to distinguish them from stored strings. Missing fields
are absent from the JSON. The child inventory lists immediate Kitchen subcollections with no
export of the other collections’ documents.
